1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the models space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 828 and the convergence rate is O(715/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 828 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the models structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 715. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
